问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，**无法确定U1700的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络**。具体分析如下： ### 1. 现有证据覆盖情况 - 仅检索到电路图中U1700的**实例存在**（证据来源：`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，实体`circuit:21:instance:U1700`），但该实例**未列出任何引脚名称、编号及其对应的网络连接**。 - 检索到的文档（如`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）和PDF（`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）中，均未包含U1700的引脚连接表或网络关联信息。 - 其他网络证据（如CAN0_INH、CAN1_INH等）均与U1700无关。 ### 2. 缺失信息 - **U1700的引脚定义**：无任何引脚名称、编号、类型或功能描述。 - **U1700的引脚网络连接**：无任何引脚对应到具体网络（如电源、地、信号等）的记录。 - **文档说明**：检索账本明确标注缺少`document_text`类型证据，且未找到任何与U1700引脚连接相关的文本描述。 ### 3. 证据冲突说明 证据中列出了多个不同的字段值（如`a4`、`b`、`b15`、`config`、`warmreset`等），但这些值来自不同文档，**无法确定其与U1700引脚或网络的对应关系**，且这些字段值本身未提供上下文，故不能作为U1700引脚连接的依据。 ### 结论 由于缺乏U1700的引脚列表及网络连接数据，**无法回答用户问题**。需要补充U1700的完整引脚功能表（datasheet或原理图引脚网络列表）才能进行具体连接分析。

power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table 7-1. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table>

问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：基于检索到的电路设计文档（来源：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF），网络VCC3V3直接连接的主要芯片（以U开头的元件）如下： 1. **U1500**：通过VIN引脚连接。 2. **U1600**：通过VCCIN引脚连接。 3. **U1700**：通过VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2等多个引脚连接。 4. **U1800**：通过VIN和MODE/SYNC引脚连接。 5. **U1802**：通过VIN引脚连接。 6. **U1804**：通过VIN引脚连接。 此外，网络VCC3V3还衍生出多个子网络（如VCC3V3_MCU、VCC3V3_ETH等），这些子网络分别连接了其他芯片，例如： - **VCC3V3_MCU**子网络连接了U1200、U1203、U1204、U1205、U1302、U1303、U1304、U1305、U503、U900C等芯片。 - **VCC3V3_ETH**子网络连接了U1500、U1501等芯片。 - **VCC3V3_EQ**子网络连接了U1802、U400-2等芯片。 **缺失信息**：当前证据仅列出了芯片的引脚连接，未提供这些芯片的具体型号或功能描述。若需确认芯片型号，需补充对应的datasheet信息。

问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：根据ADAS项目软硬件接口文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中的硬件架构章节，硬件架构部分明确列出了以下模块电路： 1. KL30电源管理电路 2. 唤醒电路 3. 电源电路（包含多个子电源转换电路，如KL30转VCC3V3、VCC3V3转各电压域等） 4. TC377最小系统 5. EQ6最小系统 6. 摄像头最小系统 7. 看门狗电路 8. 温度采样电路 9. 系统内电源电压采样电路 10. ETH电路 11. CAN/CAN FD电路 12. 3V3过压保护电路 **来源说明**：上述列表来自文档目录“2.3 模块电路 Module circuit”的章节标题（证据[2]），以及后续章节中详细描述的电路名称（如证据[6]中的“唤醒电路、电源电路、TC377最小系统、EQ6最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗电路、温度采样电路、系统内电源电压采样电源、ETH电路、CAN/CAN FD电路、3V3过压保护电路”）。 **缺失信息**：无。所有模块电路均已覆盖。 **证据冲突**：在检索到的证据中，关于电源电压精度的描述存在冲突（例如，证据[7]中MCU的VCC1V25_MCU精度标注为“1.2-1.35V”，而同一文档中其他部分可能提到3.3V电压），但由于该冲突不涉及“模块电路”的列表，在此不构成对问题的直接矛盾。若需确认具体电压值，建议进一步查阅文档中相关电源电路章节的详细描述。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。

问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：好的，我将根据您提供的证据，严格基于事实回答您的问题。 ### 0.75V电源相关芯片、输入及配置要求 根据现有证据，系统中存在两个0.75V电源轨，分别由两颗不同的芯片产生。下面将分别说明。 #### 1. VCC0V75 (用于EQ6内核供电) * **产生芯片**：该电源由 **TI的TPS62872** 电源芯片产生【11†L3-L4】【15†L16-L17】【20†L8】。 * **输入电压**：该芯片的输入电压为 **VCC3V3** (3.3V)【11†L3-L4】【15†L16-L17】【20†L8】。 * **文档配置要求**： * **使能控制**：使用MCU的 **P22.0** 引脚使能该芯片【11†L5】【15†L17】。 * **I²C通信**：通过MCU的 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 和 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 引脚进行I²C通信，以设置输出电压和进行通信【11†L5-L6】【15†L17-L18】。 * **输出电压设置**：根据芯片手册，输出电压可通过I²C接口的VSET寄存器和CONTROL2寄存器配置。VSET寄存器地址为0h【13†L4】。输出电压设定点公式取决于所选电压范围【12†L4-L7】。 * 范围1: 输出电压 = 0.4V + VSET[7:0] × 1.25 mV * 范围2: 输出电压 = 0.4V + VSET[7:0] × 2.5 mV * 范围3: 输出电压 = 0.4V + VSET[7:0] × 5 mV * 范围4: 输出电压 = 0.8V + VSET[7:0] × 10 mV * **默认电压设置**：上电时，芯片通过检测VSEL引脚的状态来确定默认输出电压和I²C目标地址【14†L7】【10†L1-L2】。 * **软启动**：芯片具有可编程的软启动时间（SSTIME[1:0]），默认值为1ms，也可设置为500μs、2ms或4ms【9†L1-L2】。 #### 2. 0V75_MIPI_DDR (用于EQ6 IO口供电) * **产生芯片**：该电源由 **矽力杰的SA23002** 电源芯片产生【17†L3-L4】【20†L14】。 * **输入电压**：该芯片的输入电压为 **VCC3V3** (3.3V)【17†L5】【20†L14】。 * **文档配置要求**： * **使能控制**：使用MCU的 **P22.1** 引脚使能该芯片【17†L6】【15†L22】。 * **其他配置**：文档中未提及SA23002芯片的其他配置要求，如输出电压设置方式等。 ### 证据冲突 在所提供的证据中，存在相互冲突的陈述，这些冲突无法被合并为确定结论，现列出如下： 1. **TPS62872 LSB检测逻辑描述冲突**：证据[9]中，对TPS62872的FSEL引脚LSB检测逻辑的描述存在矛盾： * 一处描述为：“If the MSB was 0, the circuit closes S1. If the input buffer detects a high level, LSB = 1. If the circuit detects a low level, LSB = 0.”（如果MSB为0，电路闭合S1。如果输入缓冲器检测到高电平，LSB=1。如果电路检测到低电平，LSB=0。） * 另一处描述为：“If the MSB was 1, the circuit closes S2. If the input buffer detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the circuit detects a high level, LSB = 1.”（如果MSB为1，电路闭合S2。如果输入缓冲器检测到低电平，LSB=0。如果电路检测到高电平，LSB=1。）【9†L13-L15】 * **冲突原因**：文档中对LSB检测逻辑的两种描述给出了相同的输出结果，但使用了不同的逻辑条件，这可能是文档中的笔误，导致逻辑不清晰。 2. **TPS62872 VSET寄存器电压设定点公式冲突**：在证据[12]中，VSET寄存器的电压设定点公式表述存在冲突： * “Range 4: Output voltage setpoint = 0.8 V + VSET[7:0] × 10 mV”（范围4：输出电压设定点 = 0.8V + VSET[7:0] × 10 mV）【12†L7】。 * 而其他三个范围的基准电压均为0.4V【12†L4-L6】。 * **冲突原因**：范围4的基准电压（0.8V）与其它范围（0.4V）不同，这在文档中虽被提及【14†L5】，但可能导致用户混淆。 ### 缺失信息 * 对于 **SA23002** 芯片，文档中缺少其输出电压的设置方式、输出电压精度、以及其他可能的配置要求（如软启动、时序等）。 ### 电路连接参考 根据电路设计文件，`0V75_MIPI_DDR` 电源网络通过一个模块连接（Group 18 · Power/PMIC 到 Group 6 · Power/PMIC）进行传输【8†L1】。

Note that the device always operates in DcM during the start-up ramp, regardless of other configuration settings or operating conditions. 9.3.5SwitchingFrequencySelection During device initialization, a resistor-to-digital converter in the device determines the state of the FSEL pin and sets the switching frequency of the DC/DC converter according to Table 9-2. If the circuit detects a· | During start-up, the output voltage ramps up to the target value set by the VSEL pin before ramping up or down to any new value programmed to the device over the I2C interface. 9.3.6.3Non-DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoint If none of the default voltage range or voltage setpoint combinations are suitable for the application, the user can change these device settings through I2C before the user enables the device. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。 VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | The state of the VSEL pin during power up defines the I2C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). Note that the VSEL pin also sets the default start-up voltage of the device (see Table 9-4). TI recommends that the I?C controller initiates a STOP condition on the I2C bus after the initial power up of SDA and SCL pullup voltages to ensure reset of the I?C engine. 9.5.2 Standard, Fast, Fast Mode Plus Protocol The controller initiates a data transfer by generating a start condition. The start condition is when a high-to-low transition occurs on the SDA line while SCL is high, as shown in Figure 9-15. All I?C-compatible devices must recognize a start condition. The controller then generates the SCL pulses, and transmits the 7-bit address and the read and write direction bit R/W on the SDA line. During all transmissions, the controller makes sure that data is valid. A valid data condition requires the SDA line to be stable during the entire high period of the clock pulse (see Figure 9-16). All devices recognize the address sent by the controller and compare the address to their internal fixed addresses. Only the target with a matching address generates an acknowledge (see Figure 9-17) by puling the SDA line low during the entire high period of the ninth SCL cycle. Upon detecting this acknowledge, the controller knows that a communication link with a target has been established. The controller generates further SCL cycles to either transmit data to the target (R/W bit O) or receive data from the target (R/W bit 1). In either case, the target must acknowledge the data sent by the controller. So an acknowledge signal can either be generated by the controller or by the target, depending on which one is the receiver. 9-bit valid data sequences consisting of 8-bit data and 1-bit acknowledge can continue as long as necessary. To signal the end of the data transfer, the controller generates a stop condition by pulling the SDA line from low to high while the SCL line is high (see Figure 9-15). This stop condition releases the bus and stops the communication link with the addressed target. All I2C-compatible devices must recognize the stop condition. Upon the receipt of a stop condition, all devices know that the bus is released, and they wait for a start condition followed by a matching address. | <table><caption> Table 7-1. In POR mode, no functions are available and the content of the device registers is not valid. The device leaves POR mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage increases above the POR threshold 9.4.2 Undervoltage Lockout The device operates in UVLO mode when the supply voltage is between the POR and UVLO thresholds. If the device enters UVLO mode from POR mode, no functions are available. If the device enters UVLO mode from standby mode, the output discharge function is available. The content of the device registers is valid in UVLO mode. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters POR mode when the supply voltage decreases below the POR threshold. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters standby mode when the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold. 9.4.3 Standby The device operates in standby mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold (and the device has completed its initialization) and any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register.· The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. The device initializes for 4o0 μs (typical) after the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold voltage following a device power-on reset. If the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold but not below the POR threshold, the device does not reinitialize when the supply voltage increases again. During initialization, the device reads the state of the FSEL, VSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. The following functions are available in standby mode: · I2C interface ·Output discharge ·Power good Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The device leaves standby mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves standby mode and enters on mode when all of the following conditions are true: · A high-level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. | all operating conditions. 问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的资料，以下是对您问题的回答。 ### 3.3V 电源是否有过压保护，是如何实现的？ #### 子问题 1：3.3V电源是否设计有过压保护？ **结论：是的，3.3V电源（具体为VCC3V3_MCU）设计有过压保护功能。** **来源说明：** * 证据[7]（文档 `b257f3b0795806a8`）的目录中明确指出存在一个“3V3过压保护电路”章节，表明该功能是系统设计的一部分。 * 证据[9]（文档 `6620a6e887eb13f5`）的模块功能描述中明确说明：“AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控。” 这直接证实了3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）存在过压保护。 #### 子问题 2：过压保护是如何实现的？请描述保护电路或机制。 **结论：过压保护功能是通过一个分立元件电路实现的，该电路监控VCC3V3_MCU电压，并在电压超过阈值（4.03V）时切断后级电源。** **实现机制描述：** 根据证据[9]（文档 `6620a6e887eb13f5`）的描述，该电路的工作原理如下： 1. **正常/欠压状态 (VCC3V3 < 4.03V)**：当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V时，电路中一个关键器件的`cathode`（阴极）引脚为高阻态。此时，`NMOS`（N沟道金属氧化物半导体场效应管）和`PMOS`（P沟道金属氧化物半导体场效应管）均处于关闭状态，因此`VCC3V3_SW`（后级供电输出）无电。 2. **正常工作状态 (VGS门限 < VCC3V3 < 4.03V)**：当VCC3V3电压高于`NMOS`的栅极阈值电压（VGS门限）但低于4.03V时，`NMOS`导通，进而驱动`PMOS`导通。此时，`VCC3V3_SW`与`VCC3V3`直通，为后级负载正常供电。 3. **过压保护状态 (VCC3V3 > 4.03V)**：当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，电路的`cathode`引脚输出逻辑低电平（最高1V），导致`NMOS`关闭，`PMOS`也随之关闭，`VCC3V3_SW`无电，从而切断对后级负载的供电，实现过压保护。 **电路元件：** 该电路的核心元件包括一个用于电压检测的TL431或类似精密稳压器（通过`cathode`引脚描述推断）以及用于开关控制的`NMOS`和`PMOS`。 **来源说明：** * 证据[9]（文档 `6620a6e887eb13f5`）清晰、完整地描述了上述三个工作状态，是回答此问题的关键证据。 --- ### 缺失信息 * **电路具体元件型号**：当前证据未提供该过压保护电路中使用的具体器件型号（如TL431的具体型号、MOSFET的型号）。 * **参考电路图**：虽然有电路描述，但未提供完整的电路原理图（schematic diagram）来展示各元件的具体连接方式。 * **保护范围**：该保护电路仅针对`VCC3V3_MCU`，证据中未提及系统其他3.3V电源轨（如`VCC3V3_EQ`、`VCC3V3_ETH`等）是否也有类似的独立过压保护设计。 ### 证据冲突声明 根据提供的证据质量评分，存在以下矛盾点，这些矛盾主要来源于TPS62872-Q1电源芯片的数据手册，与本题的3.3V电源过压保护电路无直接关联，但为保持信息完整性，特此列出： 1. **电压值描述冲突**：在证据[18]中，部分参数测试条件提及“Vin = 3.3 V”，而同一份文档的另一个部分（证据[18]表格内）也引用了“ViH = 2 V”。这些数值在文档上下文中可能有不同含义，但用户可能将其视为同一个字段的冲突取值。 2. **负载电流冲突**：证据[18]中，对于`VoL`（低电平输出电压）的测试条件，同时出现了“loL = 3 mA”、“loL = 5 mA”和“loL = 9 mA”三个不同的值。这分别是针对不同测试条件的参数，并非同一个电气特性下的矛盾。 3. **电阻值冲突**：证据[18]中，对于负载（Load）的描述，同时出现了“0.75 Ω”和“7.5 Ω”两种取值。这同样是针对不同测试场景（如启动和限流）的负载条件，并非矛盾。 **结论：** 上述冲突均为TPS62872-Q1数据手册中不同测试条件下的参数值，并非对同一电气特性的不同定义，不影响对3.3V电源过压保护电路实现方式的判断。

KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 注：使用本模板时请先阅读填写说明页，形成工作产品时本页不可删除变更历史Change The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. 评分上下文：6/29 段，12000/40386 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-circuit-tps22918-enumerationsuccess0.7710.5401.000not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.429not_applicable
问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：好的，基于给定的证据，以下是关于电路板上使用 TPS22918 所做电源开关的详细回答。 ### 电路板上使用 TPS22918 的电源开关实例 根据电路设计文件和文档，电路板上共有 **3 个** TPS22918 负载开关实例，其位号分别为 **U1500、U1802 和 U1803**。 --- ### 各个 TPS22918 实例的输入、输出和使能连接 以下是根据电路设计文件和文档，对每个实例的引脚连接情况进行的说明。 #### 1. 实例 U1500 (TPS22918) * **输入 (VIN)**：根据文档 [19]，其输入电源为 **VCC3V3** (系统 3.3V 电源)。 * **输出 (VOUT)**：根据文档 [19]，其输出电源为 **VCC3V3_ETH**，用于以太网 (ETH) 供电。 * **使能 (EN/ON)**：根据电路图 [8]，使能引脚（图中的 `ON` 引脚）连接到网络 **MCU_3V3_ETH_EN**。该网络连接到 MCU 的 **P21.0** 引脚，用于控制芯片的使能。 * **来源说明**： * 位号 U1500 被识别为 TPS22918 器件 [9]。 * 文档 [19] 描述了该模块的功能：将 VCC3V3 转换为 VCC3V3_ETH 用于 ETH 供电，并由 MCU 的 P21.0 引脚使能。 * 电路图 [8] 确认了使能引脚 `U1500.ON` 连接到 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 网络。 #### 2. 实例 U1802 (TPS22918) * **输入 (VIN)**：根据文档 [18]，其输入电源为 **VCC3V3** (系统 3.3V 电源)。 * **输出 (VOUT)**：根据文档 [18]，其输出电源为 **VCC3V3_EQ**，用于 EQ6 和摄像头供电。 * **使能 (EN/ON)**：根据电路图 [7]，使能引脚（图中的 `ON` 引脚）连接到网络 **MCU_3V3_EQ_EN**。该网络连接到 MCU 的 **P20.3** 引脚，用于控制芯片的使能。 * **来源说明**： * 位号 U1802 被识别为 TPS22918 器件 [10]。 * 文档 [18] 描述了该模块的功能：将 VCC3V3 转换为 VCC3V3_EQ 用于 EQ6 和摄像头供电，并由 MCU 的 P20.3 引脚使能。 * 电路图 [7] 确认了使能引脚 `U1802.ON` 连接到 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 网络。 * 文档 [20] 也确认了 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 信号由 MCU 的 P20.3 引脚输出，用于控制 VCC3V3_EQ 电源的使能。 #### 3. 实例 U1803 (TPS22918) * **输入 (VIN)**：根据文档 [17]，其输入电源为 **VCC1V8** (1.8V 电源)。 * **输出 (VOUT)**：根据文档 [17]，其输出电源为 **VCC1V8_EQ**，用于 EQ6 供电。 * **使能 (EN/ON)**：根据电路图 [6]，使能引脚（图中的 `ON` 引脚）连接到网络 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。该网络连接到 MCU 的 **P20.0** 引脚，用于控制芯片的使能。 * **来源说明**： * 位号 U1803 被识别为 TPS22918 器件 [11]。 * 文档 [17] 描述了该模块的功能：将 VCC1V8 转换为 VCC1V8_EQ 用于 EQ6 供电，并由 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚使能。 * 电路图 [6] 确认了使能引脚 `U1803.ON` 连接到 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` 网络。 --- ### 缺失信息 * **电路图引脚连接细节**：提供的电路图证据 [6, 7, 8] 仅明确显示了芯片的使能（ON）引脚连接。对于输入（VIN）和输出（VOUT）引脚的具体网络连接，电路图证据中未直接列出。本回答中关于输入/输出电源的说明，均来源于文档 [17, 18, 19] 的模块功能描述。 ### 证据冲突 根据提供的证据覆盖度报告，存在一些证据冲突，但与本次问题直接相关的电路连接信息中未发现冲突。冲突主要集中在器件手册的电气特性参数上（如 `vih` 和 `lol` 字段），这些冲突不影响本问题的回答。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | Figure 10-24. 评分上下文：8/30 段，1073/18933 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-multihop-eth-powersuccess0.4580.0000.500not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.571not_applicable
问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据现有证据，关于以太网PHY的1.0V电源供应路径，回答如下： ### 1. 以太网PHY的器件型号及其1.0V电源引脚 * **器件型号**：以太网PHY的器件型号为 **DP83TC812R**（TI公司产品）[20]。 * **1.0V电源引脚**：原理图中存在一个名为 **“VCC1V0_ETH”** 的网络，该网络连接至 **U1501** 器件的输出引脚（OUT）[6]。该网络应是为以太网PHY提供1.0V电压的电源网络。但现有证据未能明确显示该网络（VCC1V0_ETH）与PHY芯片（U1400）之间的直接连接关系。 * **来源说明**：器件型号来源于文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` [20]；电源网络连接关系来源于电路图 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` [6]。 ### 2. 该1.0V电源在电源树中的产生级及中间转换器件 **关键信息缺失**：现有证据无法提供以太网PHY的1.0V电源（VCC1V0_ETH）在电源树中的具体产生级和中间转换器件。 * **缺失细节**： * 证据提到了系统的一级电源转换（KL30转VCC3V3）[11] 以及为ETH供电的3.3V电源（VCC3V3_ETH）[12][13]。然而，**没有任何证据说明VCC1V0_ETH是由哪个电源轨（如VCC3V3_ETH、VCC3V3或其他）转换而来**。 * 证据中提到了一个名为 **U1501** 的器件，其输出为VCC1V0_ETH [6]，但未指明该器件的具体型号、功能（如LDO或DC-DC）以及输入电源网络。因此，**无法确定U1501在电源树中的具体位置和转换级**。 * 文档中描述的电源树方案（如使用LP87702 PMIC产生VCC1V8、VCC1V1等）[13] 均未提及1.0V电源的产生路径。 * **缺口说明**：由于缺乏“该1.0V电源在电源树中由哪一级产生？中间经过哪些LDO或DC-DC？”这一关键环节的证据，无法回答此问题。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F24</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>正常模式、复位模式，待机模式，断电模式，休眠模式 Normal mode, reset mode, standby mode, power off mode, sleep mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 正常模式 模块上电，正常运行模式，以太网收发正常。MDIO可以访问，Link处于连接状态。 2、 复位模式 UV或Reset event时，PHY芯片将进行复位，在Reset期间将无法LINKUP，MDIO无法总线访问 待机模式 仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：MCU外挂20MHz晶体; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：M20; None：XTAL1; None：XTAL /VEXT; None：CLOCK; None：——; None：/; None：I; None：MCU的时钟信号输入; None：I; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：M19; None：XTAL2; None：XTAL /VEXT; None：CLOCK; None：——; None：/; None：O; None：MCU的时钟驱动信号输出; None：O; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：QM; None：F09/ETH and CAN power samp2; None：MCU采样CAN的VCC50电源电压，精度要求：5V±5%; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：U10; None：AN1; None：AN1/EVADC_G0CH1/EDSADC_EDS3NA; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：VCC50_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：5V电压采样 U5V0_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 U5V0=U5V0_ACQ/分压比，分压比=1/2 精度要求：5V±10%; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：MCU采样以太网的VCC1V0_ETH电源电压 精度要求：0.9V～1.1V （仅当使用千兆以太网时采样）; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：U7; None：AN9; None：AN9/EVADC_G11CH5/EVADC_G1CH1; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：1V0_ETH_ACQ; None：以太网1V0电压采样 U1V1_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 精度：0.95~1.1V 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | None：U20; 问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：根据现有证据，主控MCU的型号为 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 **来源说明：** - 证据[1]（来自 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”，并在“模块方案”中指出“处理器...32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 - 证据[7]（同样来自该文档）的模块列表中，F01模块的功能定义直接填写为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 **缺失信息：** 无，该问题已得到充分覆盖。 **注意：** 检索到的 conflicts 字段涉及其他参数（如电压、连接器引脚等），与主控MCU型号无关，不影响本答案。
检索上下文：<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 问题：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是谁？说清楚输入、三路输出和软件配置出处。
参考答案：U1700 LP87702-Q1 从 VCC3V3 取电，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50，软件配置出处为 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我对您的问题进行回答。 ### 板上最主要的电源管理芯片 **型号：** LP87702 * **来源说明：** 证据[2] (`document:b6943ae371d76077`) 中明确提到“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案”。证据[11] (`document:44c4021cbbe6e903`) 和证据[20] (`document:e6627ad70c8cab97`) 的模块功能定义中也明确指出“LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。 ### 输入电压 * **输入电压：** VCC3V3（3.3V） * **来源说明：** 证据[2] (`document:b6943ae371d76077`) 在描述LP87702方案时，指出其输入为“VCC3V3”。证据[11] (`document:44c4021cbbe6e903`) 和证据[20] (`document:e6627ad70c8cab97`) 的表格中也明确记录了LP87702的输入为“VCC3V3电压输入”。 ### 三路输出 LP87702芯片提供三路电压输出，具体如下： | 输出轨 | 电压 | 用途 | 来源说明 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | | VCC1V8 | 1.8V | 为EQ6、CAM（摄像头）、DDR、FLASH供电 | 证据[2] (`document:b6943ae371d76077`) | | VCC1V1 | 1.1V | 为EQ6、DDR供电 | 证据[2] (`document:b6943ae371d76077`) | | VCC50 | 5.0V | 为CAN收发器供电 | 证据[2] (`document:b6943ae371d76077`) | * **补充说明：** 证据[3] (`document:ed178b5d659e251d`) 提供了各电源轨的软件监控阈值，进一步佐证了这些输出轨的存在。例如，VCC1V8的软件阈值范围为1.613V~2.012V，VCC1V1为0.993V~1.253V，VCC50为4.379V~5.721V。 ### 软件配置出处 LP87702的软件配置说明位于以下文档中： * **文档名称：** `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` * **具体章节：** 证据[5] (`document:58106bd2421dc2d1`) 和证据[6] (`document:d69d04d302326d46`) 的目录结构显示，在“7. 其他芯片配置 Other IC Configuration”章节下，包含“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节。此小节专门描述了LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器。 * **通信机制：** 证据[10] (`document:bcb07de62999159b`) 提供了LP87702的I2C通信参数：速率为230KHz，I2C地址为0x08。 * **控制方式：** 证据[2] (`document:b6943ae371d76077`) 和证据[19] (`document:a6f318d1053a4c7e`) 描述了控制方式：MCU通过GPIO引脚P15.3使能VCC50输出，通过I2C（引脚MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5）设置VCC1.8V和VCC1.1V的输出电压和使能。 ### 缺失信息 * **输出电流：** 所有证据中均未明确提及LP87702各路输出的具体电流能力（如最大输出电流）。 * **输入电压范围：** 证据中仅说明输入为“VCC3V3”，但未提及LP87702芯片自身的输入电压范围（如最小/最大允许输入电压）。 * **详细的寄存器配置表：** 证据中虽然提到了“Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”，但未提供该表格的具体内容，因此无法得知详细的寄存器设置值。 ### 证据冲突声明 在提供的证据中，存在关于“TCAN1044A”和“TCAN1145”的字段冲突，已被记录在`coverage_matrix.conflicts`中。此冲突是关于CAN收发器芯片，与您所问的“板上最主要的电源管理芯片（LP87702）”**无关**，因此不影响上述答案。
检索上下文：<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>电源轨</td><td colspan='2'>软件设置阈值</td></tr><tr><td>电源轨</td><td>Min</td><td>Max</td></tr><tr><td>VCC3V3_EQ</td><td>2.884</td><td>3.782</td></tr><tr><td>VCC1V8</td><td>1.613</td><td>2.012</td></tr><tr><td>VCC1V8_EQ</td><td>1.613</td><td>2.012</td></tr><tr><td>VCC1V1</td><td>0.993</td><td>1.253</td></tr><tr><td>VCC0V75</td><td>0.673</td><td>0.836</td></tr><tr><td>0V75_MIPI_DDR</td><td>0.658</td><td>0.842</td></tr><tr><td>VCC3V3_CAM</td><td>2.884</td><td>3.782</td></tr><tr><td>VCC1V8_CAM</td><td>1.603</td><td>2.023</td></tr><tr><td>VCC1V1_CAM</td><td>0.9335</td><td>1.284</td></tr><tr><td>VCC50</td><td>4.379</td><td>5.721</td></tr><tr><td>VCC3V3_ETH</td><td>2.731</td><td>3.961</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode With this safety mechanism all the registers can only be written by selected masters. Write accesses from forbidden masters are blocked and cause an Alarm. Note: Alarms or Interrupts from Safety Mechanisms which control Software Failures (e.g. access protection, MPU) shall not be ignored, but treated according to application needs. If application is not intended to violate access protection, a reasonable Alarm or Interrupt reaction could be application reset to control the software causing the violation. One violation per driving cycle shall be assumed as normal behaviour (caused by soft errors or unintended access protection violations). Alternatively, the application could execute cyclic (once per DTI) software checks to verify that the configured safety mechanisms are effective and functional.; Unnamed: 3：nan; Unnamed: 4：nan; Unnamed: 5：nan; Unnamed: 6：SMU Alarm ALM7[17] - SRI0 Bus Error Event SMU Alarm ALM7[18] - SRI1 Bus Error Event SMU Alarm ALM7[19] - SRI2 Bus Error Event; Unnamed: 7：nan; Unnamed: 8：nan; Unnamed: 9：nan; Unnamed: 10：nan; Unnamed: 11：nan; Unnamed: 12：nan; Unnamed: 13：nan; Unnamed: 14：nan; Unnamed: 15：nan ——12.1TC3XXX_SM | Unnamed: 0：2.5 MCU Function - MCU Interconnect; Unnamed: 1：nan; Unnamed: 2：nan; Unnamed: 3：nan; Unnamed: 4：nan; Unnamed: 5：nan; Unnamed: 6：nan; Unnamed: 7：nan; Unnamed: 8：nan; Unnamed: 9：nan; Unnamed: 10：nan; Unnamed: 11：nan; Unnamed: 12：nan; Unnamed: 13：nan; Unnamed: 14：nan; Unnamed: 15：nan ——12.1TC3XXX_SM Unnamed: 0：2.5.1 SRI; Unnamed: 1：nan; Unnamed: 2：nan; Unnamed: 3：nan; Unnamed: 4：nan; Unnamed: 5：nan; Unnamed: 6：nan; Unnamed: 7：nan; Unnamed: 8：nan; Unnamed: 9：nan; Unnamed: 10：nan; Unnamed: 11：nan; Unnamed: 12：nan; Unnamed: 13：nan; Unnamed: 14：nan; Unnamed: 15：nan ——12.1TC3XXX_SM Unnamed: 0：SM[HW]:SRI:SRI_ADDRESS_MAP_MONITORING; Unnamed: 1：6.476; Unnamed: 2：A slave bus agent provides the capability to decode if an incoming bus transaction address belongs to the agent address space.
