For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据现有证据，对 U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能控制和 I²C 控制网络分析如下。**请注意，所有子问题的证据覆盖度均为“部分”（partial），存在明确的缺失信息和证据冲突，以下结论不可视为完整确定信息。** --- ### 1. U1800 的输入信号 **已知信息：** - U1800 是电源转换芯片 TPS62872（来自证据[15]的描述），其输入电源为 VCC3V3（3.3 V），输出为 VCC0V75（0.75 V）。 - 芯片的输入引脚包括 VIN（电源输入）、EN（使能引脚）、VSEL（启动电压和 I²C 地址选择）、FSEL（开关频率选择）、SCL/SDA（I²C 接口）等，这些是 TPS62872 数据手册中描述的通用引脚。 - 在电路中，U1800 的使能信号由 MCU 的 GPIO 引脚 `MCU_VCC0V75_EN`（对应 MCU 引脚 P22.0）控制（证据[15]）。 **缺失信息：** - 电路图中 U1800 的具体引脚网络连接（如 VIN、VSEL、FSEL、SCL、SDA 等引脚的连接对象）未提供。 - 输入信号的类型（如电压范围、电流需求）和详细定义缺失。 - 缺少文档（如数据手册表格）或电路图（circuit:21:instance:U1800 未提供具体引脚列表）来完整描述所有输入信号。 **来源：** 证据[15]（文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）提到 F14 模块由 TPS62872 实现 VCC3V3 转 VCC0V75，并由 MCU 控制使能。证据[1][2][6][19]等提供了 TPS62872 数据手册的通用引脚描述，但未绑定到 U1800 的具体电路。 --- ### 2. U1800 的输出检测功能 **已知信息：** - TPS62872 芯片内置窗口比较器，用于监控输出电压：当输出电压低于标称值的 95% 或高于 105% 时，比较器输出高电平，经过 40 μs 去毛刺后驱动开漏 PG 引脚（证据[1]）。 - 输出检测通过 PG 引脚指示，但 PG 引脚在以下情况下会被强制拉低：热关断、器件禁用、欠压锁定、软启动期间（证据[1]）。 - 当 DVS（动态电压调节）激活时，可通过 CONTROL3 寄存器的 PGBLNKDVS 位禁用 PG 比较器（证据[1]）。 **缺失信息：** - 未明确 U1800 的 PG 引脚在电路中的连接方式（例如是否连接到 MCU 或上拉电阻等）。 - 未提供 U1800 的具体输出检测电路拓扑（如是否使用了远程感测引脚 VOSNS/GOSNS，证据[1]提到远程感测功能，但未说明 U1800 是否启用）。 - 缺少文档或电路图说明 U1800 的输出检测功能在硬件上是如何实现的。 **来源：** 证据[1]（文档 FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf）描述了 TPS62872 的窗口比较器和 PG 引脚行为，但未涉及 U1800 的具体电路连接。 --- ### 3. U1800 的使能控制 **已知信息：** - U1800 的使能由 MCU 的 GPIO 信号 `MCU_VCC0V75_EN`（引脚 P22.0）控制，该信号为高电平时使能 VCC0V75 电源输出，低电平时关断（证据[15]）。 - 芯片的 EN 引脚具有精确阈值：上升阈值 1.0 V，下降阈值 0.9 V，容差 ±30 mV（证据[19]）。 - 初始化时，芯片会先拉低 EN 引脚，完成初始化后释放 EN 引脚，使外部电路控制使能（证据[19]）。在非堆叠应用中，可通过设置 CONTROL3 寄存器的 SINGLE=1 禁用 EN 引脚的下拉（证据[19]）。 **缺失信息：** - 未提供 U1800 的 EN 引脚是否直接连接到 `MCU_VCC0V75_EN` 信号，还是通过其他电路（如分压、缓冲）连接。 - 缺少使能逻辑的详细时序（如软启动时间由 SSTIME 寄存器设置，但未给出 U1800 的具体配置）。 - 文档（如数据手册）或电路图未提供使能引脚的外部电路细节。 **来源：** 证据[15]（文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）明确描述了使能控制信号。证据[19]（文档 FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf）提供了 EN 引脚的行为描述。 --- ### 4. U1800 的 I²C 控制网络 **已知信息：** - TPS62872 支持 I²C 兼容接口，用于控制、监测和警告功能，包括输出电压调节、状态读取等（证据[6]）。 - I²C 地址由 VSEL 引脚在上电时的状态决定（证据[17]），且与启动电压关联（证据[8]）。 - 寄存器包括：VSET（地址 0h，输出电压设定）、CONTROL1（1h）、CONTROL2（2h，含电压范围和软启动时间）、CONTROL3（3h，含 DVS 相关位）、STATUS（4h，含状态位）（证据[13]）。 - I²C 通信需要 SCL 和 SDA 信号线，并遵循标准协议（开始、地址、读写位、数据、应答、停止）（证据[9][17]）。 **缺失信息：** - 未提供 U1800 的具体 I²C 地址（取决于 VSEL 引脚连接，但未给出 VSEL 状态）。 - 未说明 U1800 的 SCL 和 SDA 引脚在电路中的网络连接（例如连接到 MCU 的哪个 I²C 总线，是否与其它器件共享）。 - 缺少 I²C 寄存器配置的详细内容（如 U1800 使用的默认输出电压、软启动时间等）。 - 文档或电路图未提供 I²C 控制网络的完整拓扑。 **来源：** 证据[6][8][9][13][17]（文档 FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf）描述了 TPS62872 的 I²C 接口和寄存器，但未绑定到 U1800 的具体电路。 --- ### 证据冲突列表 根据检索账本中的 conflicts 字段，存在以下冲突： | 冲突字段 | 冲突值 | 来源 | 说明 | |----------|--------|------|------| | lsb | 0. 和 1. | 同一 PDF 文件（FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf） | 描述 FSEL 引脚检测 LSB 时，不同位置给出了不同取值，可能对应不同检测条件（MSB 为 0 或 1），但未明确上下文。 | | none | 大量不同值（如 "1", "1v0_eth_acq", "5v", "a7", "adc", ...） | 文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx | 这些值似乎是不同字段的枚举值，但被归为同一字段，可能为数据提取错误导致。 | | hw interface component | "b" 和 "qm" | 同一文档 | 涉及不同模块的硬件接口组件分类，但未明确冲突原因。 | | ecu connector | "x1900-15", "x2000-54", "x2000-57" | 同一文档 | 不同 ECU 连接器编号，可能对应不同信号，但被归为同一字段。 | | load | "0.75 ω", "7.5", "7.5 ω" | 同一 PDF | 负载值描述不一致，可能为不同测试条件（如负载电阻或无单位数值）。 | | default setting | "1" 和 "off" | 同一 PDF | 可能对应不同寄存器的默认值，无法合并。 | **注：** 这些冲突均未直接涉及 U1800 的输入/输出检测、使能或 I²C 控制的具体参数，但表明证据中存在不一致的数据，无法作为可靠结论使用。 --- ### 总结 - **输入信号**：已知 VIN（3.3V）、EN（由 MCU_VCC0V75_EN 控制），但引脚连接和完整定义缺失。 - **输出检测**：PG 引脚功能已知，但 U1800 的具体连接和配置未提供。 - **使能控制**：使能信号已明确，但电路细节和时序配置缺失。 - **I²C 控制网络**：I²C 接口功能已知，但地址、信号线连接和寄存器配置缺失。 - **证据冲突**：存在多个字段的冲突值，无法直接使用。 要获取完整信息，需要提供 U1800 的电路图详细引脚连接、数据手册中针对此具体型号的寄存器配置、以及描述 I²C 地址和唤醒机制的文档表格。
检索上下文：Instance U1800: TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1, 202308165398, TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1, PQFN50P355X255X105-16. | window comparator monitors the output voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | td(RAMP) = 500 μs td(RAMP) = 1 ms (default) td(RAMP) = 2 ms td(RAMP) = 4 ms· The device ignores new values until the soft-start sequence is complete if the user programs the following when the device soft-start sequence has already started: A new output voltage setpoint (VOuT[7:o]) An output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]) Soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings If the user change the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, the device ramps up or down to the new value. The device can start up into a prebiased output. In this case, only a portion of the internal voltage ramp is seen externally (see Figure 9-9). Note that the device always operates in DcM during the start-up ramp, regardless of other configuration settings or operating conditions. 9.3.5SwitchingFrequencySelection During device initialization, a resistor-to-digital converter in the device determines the state of the FSEL pin and sets the switching frequency of the DC/DC converter according to Table 9-2. For a reliable voltage setting, ensure there is no stray current path connected to the FSEL pin and that the1. parasitic capacitance between the FSEL pin and GND is less than 1o0 pF. identical circuit detects the state of the VSEL pin - see Section 9.3.6.2). Detection of the state of the FSEL pin works as follows: To detect the most significant bit (MSB), the circuit opens S1 and S2, and the input buffer detects if a high or a low level is connected to the FSEL pin. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | <table><caption> Table 9-15. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. The user cannot select the start-up voltage and I2C address independently.(1) The user can use the VSEL pin to select which of the four start-up voltages the device uses. (3) | Attempting to read data from register addresses not listed in this section will result in Oxoo being read out. 9.5.3 I2c Update Sequence The following are required for a single update: · A start condition · A valid I2C address A register address byte· · A data byte After the receipt of each byte, the receiving device acknowledges by pulling the SDA line low during the high period of a single clock pulse. A valid I2C address selects the target. The target performs an update on the falling edge of the acknowledge signal that follows the LSB byte. "0"Write A = Acknowledge (SDA low) A = Not acknowledge (SDA high) S = START condition Sr = REPEATED START condition P = STOP condition "0" Write I"Read A=Acknowledge(SDAlow) A=Notacknowledge(SDAhigh) S= START condition Sr=REPEATEDSTARTcondition P= STOP condition 9.5.412cRegisterReset The I2C registers can be reset by: Pulling the input voltage below 1.4 V (typical)· Setting the RESET bit in the CONTROL register. When RESET = 1, all registers are reset to their default values and a new start-up begins immediately. After t(En), you can program the I2C registers again. 9.6 Register Map locations. Note that the VSEL pin also sets the default start-up voltage of the device (see Table 9-4). TI recommends that the I?C controller initiates a STOP condition on the I2C bus after the initial power up of SDA and SCL pullup voltages to ensure reset of the I?C engine. 9.5.2 Standard, Fast, Fast Mode Plus Protocol The controller initiates a data transfer by generating a start condition. The start condition is when a high-to-low transition occurs on the SDA line while SCL is high, as shown in Figure 9-15. All I?C-compatible devices must recognize a start condition. The controller then generates the SCL pulses, and transmits the 7-bit address and the read and write direction bit R/W on the SDA line. During all transmissions, the controller makes sure that data is valid. A valid data condition requires the SDA line to be stable during the entire high period of the clock pulse (see Figure 9-16). All devices recognize the address sent by the controller and compare the address to their internal fixed addresses. Only the target with a matching address generates an acknowledge (see Figure 9-17) by puling the SDA line low during the entire high period of the ninth SCL cycle. Upon detecting this acknowledge, the controller knows that a communication link with a target has been established. The controller generates further SCL cycles to either transmit data to the target (R/W bit O) or receive data from the target (R/W bit 1). In either case, the target must acknowledge the data sent by the controller. So an acknowledge signal can either be generated by the controller or by the target, depending on which one is the receiver. 9-bit valid data sequences consisting of 8-bit data and 1-bit acknowledge can continue as long as necessary. To signal the end of the data transfer, the controller generates a stop condition by pulling the SDA line from low to high while the SCL line is high (see Figure 9-15). This stop condition releases the bus and stops the communication link with the addressed target. All I2C-compatible devices must recognize the stop condition. Upon the receipt of a stop condition, all devices know that the bus is released, and they wait for a start condition followed by a matching address. | 10.3 Best Design Practices Figure 10-22.Shutdown (Discharge Enabled) 10.2.3 Application Curves (continued) 100MS/s 250MS/'s 3.30 3.30 V →3.0000ms T+600.000s Load = 0.75 Ω Load = 7.5 Q Figure 10-20. Current Limit (Hiccup) INCORRECT CORRECT TPS6287x-Q1 TPS6287x-Q1 2.7 V to 6 V 2.7Vto6 V VIN ≥ 15 kQ EN INCORRECT CORRECT TPS6287x-Q1 TPS6287x-Q1 2.7V to 6 V 2.7V to 6 V VIN ≥15 kΩ ENABLE ENABLE EN | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | None：EYEQ6L 边界位置检测输出; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：A7; None：P10.0; None：P10.0/GTM_TIM4_IN0_12/GTM_TIM1_IN4_2/GTM_TIM0_IN4_2/GETH_RXERC/GTM_TOUT102/QSPI1_SLSO10; None：MCU采样以太网的VCC1V0_ETH电源电压 精度要求：0.9V～1.1V （仅当使用千兆以太网时采样）; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：U7; None：AN9; None：AN9/EVADC_G11CH5/EVADC_G1CH1; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：1V0_ETH_ACQ; None：以太网1V0电压采样 U1V1_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 精度：0.95~1.1V 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | <table><caption> *All dimensics are nominal</caption> <tr><th >Device</th><th >Package</th><th 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：### 回答 #### 子问题1：从VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源路径上经过哪颗负载开关？ **答案**：经过一颗型号为 **TPS22918** 的负载开关。 **来源说明**： - 文档[17]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中明确描述：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.” - 文档[18]（同一文档的F19模块描述）进一步说明：“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。” **缺失信息**：无。 --- #### 子问题2：该负载开关的使能信号是什么？ **答案**：使能信号为 **MCU的P20.0引脚**（GPIO输出，高电平使能）。 **来源说明**： - 文档[17]（同上）指出：“用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” - 文档[18]（同上）同样明确：“用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” - 结合文档[16]中类似电源使能信号的描述（如“高：使能VCC3V3_EQ电源，低：关断”），可推断P20.0引脚输出高电平时使能TPS22918，低电平时关断。 **缺失信息**：无。 --- ### 补充说明 - **证据冲突**：检索账本中列出的部分冲突（如“hw interface component”字段取值“b”和“qm”等）与当前问题无关，不影响上述结论。若需确认具体引脚电平逻辑，建议参考TPS22918数据手册或MCU GPIO配置文档。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F17</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH is used for ETH power supply, and the load switching scheme is now selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_ETH，提供给MCU的ETH供电。P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-ETH to supply power to ETH of the MCU. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我将严格基于检索到的信息，按子问题组织回答。 ### 子问题1：该文档中声明了哪些CAN相关模块？ 根据文档证据，板上声明了以下4个CAN相关模块： * **CAN0**：模块序号F23，基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯 [19]。 * **CAN1**：模块序号F22，基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯 [19]。 * **CAN2**：模块序号F21，基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯 [19]。 * **CAN3**：模块序号F20，基于TCAN1145的CAN通讯，硬件支持特定报文唤醒 [19]。 **来源说明**：以上信息来自文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的模块列表 [19] 和模块功能描述 [20]。 ### 子问题2：根据网表，这些CAN相关模块的具体连接是什么？ 可以，根据网表证据，可以确认这些CAN模块的具体网络连接。以下列出各模块相关的关键网络及其连接情况： **CAN0 模块 (F23)** * **CAN0数据总线**: * `CAN0H`: 连接到 C1201, D1200, L1200, R1209, R1214, TP1206, X1900 (连接器) [1]。 * `CAN0L`: 连接到 C1200, D1200, L1200, R1212, R1215, TP1215, X1900 (连接器) [2]。 * **CAN0控制与状态**: * `CAN0_INH`: 连接到 D1203, R1630, TP1219, U1200, U1203 [3]。 * `CAN0_INH_ACQ`: 连接到 C1216, R1236, R1237, TP1232, U900A (MCU) [4]。 * `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`: 连接到 R1126, R1228, R1241, TP1217, U1200, U1202, U1203 [5]。 **CAN1 模块 (F22)** * **CAN1数据总线**: * `CAN1H`: 连接到 C1207, D1201, L1201, R1220, R1224, TP1222, X1900 (连接器) [6]。 * `CAN1L`: 连接到 C1206, D1201, L1201, R1223, R1225, TP1223, X1900 (连接器) [7]。 * **CAN1控制与状态**: * `CAN1_INH`: 连接到 D1204, R1629, TP1228, U1204, U1205 [8]。 **CAN2 模块 (F21)** * **CAN2数据总线**: * `CAN2H`: 连接到 C1301, D1300, L1300, R1302, R1306, TP1301, X1900 (连接器) [9]。 * `CAN2L`: 连接到 C1300, D1300, L1300, R1305, R1307, TP1303, X1900 (连接器) [10]。 * **CAN2控制与状态**: * `CAN2_INH`: 连接到 D1303, R1628, TP1314, U1302, U1303 [11]。 * `CAN2_INH_ACQ`: 连接到 C1314, R1329, R1330, TP1321, U900A (MCU) [12]。 **CAN3 模块 (F20)** * **CAN3数据总线**: * `CAN3H`: 连接到 C1306, D1301, L1301, R1311, R1315, TP1307, X1900 (连接器) [13]。 * `CAN3L`: 连接到 C1305, D1301, L1301, R1314, R1316, TP1309, X1900 (连接器) [14]。 * **CAN3控制与状态**: * `CAN3_INH`: 连接到 D1304, R1609, TP1319, U1304, U1305 [15]。 * `CAN3_INH_ACQ`: 连接到 C1315, R1331, R1332, TP1322, U900A (MCU) [16]。 **来源说明**：以上所有网络连接信息均来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 的网表 [1-16]。此外，文档 [20] 还提供了这些模块与MCU (U900A) 之间的引脚连接信息，例如CAN0的发送引脚连接到MCU的 `P13.0`，接收引脚连接到 `P13.1` 等。 ### 缺失信息 * 在回答“具体连接”时，**缺失预期的表格类证据**（`spreadsheet_table`）来汇总所有连接。当前的网表证据（`circuit_design`）虽然提供了详细连接，但未以表格形式呈现。 ### 证据冲突 检索结果中存在多处证据冲突，主要是由于文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中不同部分对同一模块的描述不一致： 1. **芯片型号冲突**：关于CAN模块使用的芯片，存在冲突。 * 文档 [19] 明确声明 **CAN0、CAN1、CAN2** 基于 **TCAN1044A**，**CAN3** 基于 **TCAN1145**。 * 然而，文档 [18] 和 [20] 将 **F20/F21/F22/F23** 四个CAN模块合并描述，并指出它们都兼容 **TCAN1044A** 和 **TCAN1145** 两种芯片，根据客户需求选贴。 * **结论**：关于每个CAN模块具体使用的是哪种芯片，不同证据存在矛盾。无法确定是固定使用其中一种，还是可以选贴。 2. **寄存器配置值冲突**：在描述 **TCAN1145** 芯片的工作模式时，文档 [18] 中给出了相互矛盾的寄存器配置值。例如，对于“Normal模式”，同一份文档内同时提到了 `MC=111` 和 `MC=100` 两种值。 * **结论**：关于TCAN1145进入Normal模式和Standby模式所需的SPI寄存器 `MC` 的具体配置值，文档内部存在冲突，无法确定准确值。 **注意**：以上冲突均来自同一份文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的不同部分，这表明该文档自身存在不一致性。

of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, now choose LP87702 PMIC scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，其中VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片。 LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50, in which VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR and FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6 and DDR power supply, and VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supplyP15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1 output voltage and enable it. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F02/F05/F10</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>对EQ6和LPDDR4周边PCB温度进行监控。AOU需求MCU监控EQ6芯片内结温，确保EQ6工作安全。AOU需求EQ6监控CAM的工作温度，要求温度监控功能达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统的功能安全。 The PCB temperature around EQ6 and LPDDR4 was monitored. AOU requires MCU to monitor the junction temperature inside EQ6 chip to ensure the safety of EQ6. AOU requires EQ6 to monitor the operating temperature of the CAM, and the temperature monitoring function is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the functional safety of the EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6温度采样 EQ6-L内部内置了两个温度传感器，并通过外部SPI接口实时发送给MCU，硬件设计可以支持MCU对EQ6结温的监控。 1, EQ6 temperature sampling EQ6-l has two internal temperature sensors, which are sent to the MCU in real time through the external SPI interface. The hardware design can support the MCU to monitor the junction temperature of EQ6. NTC温度采样 硬件设计使用温敏电阻NTC（NCU15XH103F6SRC）采集EQ6/LPDDR4周边环境温度，并通过MCU进行ADC采样和实时监控。NTC温度监控范围：-40~150℃，监控精度±3℃@-40~150℃，MCU的ADC为10bit，即1024个量阶，使得监测精度可以达到3.2mV，电压采集范围0-3.3V。用MCU的AN7引脚采样。 2.NTC temperature sampling In hardware design, the temperature sensor NTC (NCU15XH103F6SRC) is used to collect the ambient temperature of EQ6/LPDDR4, and the ADC sampling and real-time monitoring are carried out by MCU. NTC temperature monitoring range: -40-150℃, monitoring accuracy ±3℃@-40-150℃, MCU ADC is 10bit, that is, 1024 quantity order, so that the monitoring accuracy can reach 3.2mV, voltage acquisition range 0-3.3V. The AN7 pin of the MCU is used for sampling. CAM温度采样 CAM的IMX-728内置了两个温度传感器，温度测量范围是：-40~125℃，温度监控精度为±5℃@-40~125℃。EYEQ6可以通过I2C接口（EQ6L的I2C_0_SCLK和I2C_0_SDA引脚）实时读取IMX-728的工作温度，并进行状态监控和异常诊断。 3.CAM temperature sampling CAM IMX-728 built-in two temperature sensors, temperature measurement range is: -40~125℃, temperature monitoring accuracy of ±5℃@-40~125℃. EYEQ6 can read the operating temperature of the IMX-728 in real time through the I2C interface (I2C_0_SCLK and I2C_0_SDA pins of EQ6L), and perform condition monitoring and anomaly diagnosis. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F24</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>正常模式、复位模式，待机模式，断电模式，休眠模式 Normal mode, reset mode, standby mode, power off mode, sleep mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 正常模式 模块上电，正常运行模式，以太网收发正常。MDIO可以访问，Link处于连接状态。 2、 复位模式 UV或Reset event时，PHY芯片将进行复位，在Reset期间将无法LINKUP，MDIO无法总线访问 待机模式 仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | ECU Connector：X1900-12 X1900-4; None：CAN2L CAN2H; None：CAN Communction; HW Interface Component ：QM; None：F21/CAN2; None：CAN2外挂TCAN1044A收发器，支持速率为： CAN：500Kbps CANFD：2/5/8Mbps 支持兼容TCAN1145/TJA1043; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：B16; None：P14.0; None：P14.0/GTM_TIM1_IN3_5/GTM_TIM0_IN3_5/GTM_TOUT80/ERAY0_TXDA/ERAY0_TXDB/CAN01_TXD; None：CAN01_TXD; None：MCU_CAN2_TXD_P14.0; None：/; None：O; None：CAN2信号输出; None：HI-Z，TCAN1044A有内置硬件上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：A15; None：P14.1; None：P14.1/GTM_TIM1_IN4_3/GTM_TIM0_IN4_3/CAN01_RXDB/SCU_E_REQ3_1/PMS_PINAWKP/GTM_TOUT81; None：CAN01_RXDB; None：MCU_CAN2_RXD_P14.1; None：/; None：I; None：CAN2信号输入; None：HI-Z,硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：QM; None：J2; None：P00.5; None：P00.5/GTM_TIM3_IN4_1/GTM_TIM3_IN0_11/CAN11_RXDB/GTM_TOUT14/QSPI3_SLSO3//EVADC_G9CH7; None：GPIO/QSPI3_SLSO3; None：CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5; None：/; None：O; None：当贴TCAN1044A时：CAN2待机控制信号： 高：进入STANDBY模式 低：退出STANDBY模式 当贴TCAN1145时：CAN2 SPI3片选信号： 高：释放设备 低：选中设备; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><caption> Table 7-1. 问题：仅根据网表，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值是多少？
参考答案：网表只能表明连接拓扑，不能给出精确保护阈值；当前缺少对应数据手册或规格证据，应明确无法确认。
实际回答：根据您提供的网表证据，**无法确定3V3过压保护的精确触发阈值**。 **子问题：在网表文档中，3V3过压保护的触发阈值是多少？** * **状态：** 证据不足 * **证据分析：** 提供的证据均为网表（circuit_design）中的网络连接信息，例如 `VCC3V3`、`VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 等。这些信息仅描述了电路中各网络与器件的连接关系，**不包含任何关于过压保护功能的触发阈值（如电压值）**。 * **缺失信息：** 过压保护的触发阈值（例如，具体的电压数值，如3.6V或3.63V等）未在网表中给出。该信息通常需要在器件的**数据手册（datasheet）** 或电路设计的**原理图（schematic）** 中的电源管理部分查找。 * **结论：** 仅凭现有网表证据，无法回答该问题。 **建议：** 请查阅相关电源管理芯片或保护器件的**数据手册**，或电路原理图中与3V3电源相关的保护电路部分，以获取精确的过压保护触发阈值。

<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | <table><tr><td>序号</td><td>模块序号</td><td>模块名称</td><td>功能定义</td><td>功能安全需求</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>F013</td><td>POWER</td><td>LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample1</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>F14</td><td>POWER</td><td>TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出，EQ6L核电供电</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>F15</td><td>POWER</td><td>SA23002，VCC3V3输入，0V75_MIPI_DDR电压输出</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>4</td><td>F16</td><td>POWER</td><td>TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC3V3_EQ输出</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>4</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>4</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr></table> | Figure 9-14. 评分上下文：0/0 段，0/0 字符；已裁剪：否
hw-v1-circuit-vcc3v3-rolesuccessfailed0.5241.000not_applicable0.5001.0001.0000.233not_applicable
问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：基于提供的证据，关于VCC3V3在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电，回答如下： ### **VCC3V3网络主要供电的电源器件** 根据证据，VCC3V3（由一级DC-DC基于LN10046芯片从12V转换而来）是系统的主要电源轨，它为板上多个后续的电源转换器件（LDO、DC-DC、负载开关）提供输入电压，这些器件再将电压转换为其他电源域。具体的主要电源器件如下： 1. **MCU（微控制器）的电源转换**： * VCC3V3经过磁珠转换为VCC3V3_MCU，用于给MCU的3.3V电源域（如VDDP3、VEXT）供电。该过程由LN10046这一级DC-DC直接产生，但MCU的供电路径经过磁珠，因此VCC3V3是VCC3V3_MCU的来源。 * **来源**：文档[10]（F11/F12...模块）和文档[11]（MCU Component接口描述）。 2. **EQ6（图像处理SOC）的电源系统**： * VCC3V3是多个为EQ6供电的电源转换芯片的输入源： * **VCC3V3转VCC0V75**：用于EQ6的内核供电，方案为TI的TPS62872 DC-DC芯片。 * **VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR**：用于EQ6的IO口供电，方案为矽力杰的SA23002 LDO/电源芯片。 * **VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50**：用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH及CAN收发器供电，方案为LP87702 PMIC。 * **VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ**：用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，方案为TPS22918负载开关。 * **来源**：文档[10]（F11/F12...模块）、文档[13]（F15模块）、文档[14]（F16模块）、文档[15]（F14模块）。 3. **以太网（ETH）供电**： * VCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，方案为TPS22918负载开关。 * **来源**：文档[10]（F11/F12...模块）、文档[16]（F17模块）。 4. **其他电源器件**： * 根据电路设计图，VCC3V3还直接连接到多个电源芯片的输入引脚（VIN），例如： * U1500（可能为ETH相关的电源芯片） * U1600 * U1700 * U1800, U1802, U1804 * **来源**：电路设计证据[2]。 ### **缺失信息说明** * 证据中未明确区分所有连接至VCC3V3的电源器件的具体型号和功能（例如U1600、U1700等），仅能确认其连接关系。用户问题中“忽略电阻电容”的要求已得到满足，证据中列出的主要负载均为电源芯片或功能模块的电源输入引脚。 ### **证据冲突** * **无**：在VCC3V3主要供电给哪些电源器件这一核心问题上，电路设计证据和文档证据不存在冲突。冲突列表中列出的不同值（如1v25, 3v3, a19, b等）主要出现在不同文档片段中对同一字段（如HM ID、引脚号）的描述，不涉及VCC3V3的供电对象，因此不影响结论。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F15</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用SA23002电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为0V75_MIPI_DDR，提供给EQ6的IO口供电。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 SA23002 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR to supply power to the I/O port of EQ6. 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：根据HSI文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的目录及内容，软件配置LP87702应查阅**第7.2节“LP87702配置”（LP87702 Configuration）**，该节隶属于“7 其他芯片配置（Other IC Configuration）”。该节明确列出了LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器（参见证据[2][3]）。 **来源说明**： - 证据[2]（文档块d69d04d302326d46）中直接列出“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节。 - 证据[3]（文档块58106bd2421dc2d1）的目录显示“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”位于第72页。 **有关证据冲突**： 在检索到的证据中存在若干字段冲突（详见coverage_matrix.conflicts），例如： - 同一字段在不同文档片段中取值不同（如“operation disabled 1b”字段在PDF中分别出现“hiccup”和“ssc”值）。 - 表格列跨度（td colspan）取值不一致（2、3、4）。 - 部分信号名称、引脚编号等字段存在重复或变体。 这些冲突主要出现在TPS62872相关文档（FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf）以及HSI文档的表格中，但**不影响LP87702配置章节的定位**。对于LP87702软件配置章节，所有支持证据均一致指向第7.2节。 **缺失信息**：无。子问题sq_1已完全覆盖，无需额外补充。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：根据现有证据，网表中出现的“TC367”和接口文档中出现的“TC377”是两个不同的芯片型号，且当前证据**无法确定**两者是同一芯片的不同版本、替代关系还是笔误。同时，BOM表和设计变更记录中**未发现**明确的型号变更通知。 以下是按子问题组织的详细分析： ### 子问题 1：网表中TC367出现在哪个具体电路或模块？上下文是否有其他标识？ - **结论**：TC367 出现在网表（电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）中，具体涉及与 JTAG 调试接口和复位相关的网络。上下文明确标识了该芯片的 JTAG 网络（如 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`）和复位信号 `TC367_RESET_N`。 - **来源说明**： - 证据 [1]~[6] 显示，网表中存在多个以 `TC367_` 开头的网络，例如 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_RESET_N` 等，这些网络连接到编号为 `X2000` 的器件上。 - **缺失信息**：网表中未直接标注该芯片的完整型号（如 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`），仅以“TC367”作为网络前缀。 ### 子问题 2：接口文档中TC377出现在哪个规格或引脚定义部分？上下文是否明确指向同一芯片？ - **结论**：接口文档明确将主控芯片定义为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`，并确认其为 `TC377` 系列芯片。上下文指向的是同一颗芯片，用于ADAS产品的MCU最小系统。 - **来源说明**： - 证据 [8] 明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**主控芯片”。 - 证据 [9] 和 [14] 的模块列表（F01）中，将模块名称定义为“MCU”，功能定义明确为“**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**”。 - **缺失信息**：接口文档中未提及“TC367”型号，也未对网表中的TC367命名做出解释。 ### 子问题 3：官方数据手册中TC367和TC377的关系是什么？是同一芯片的不同版本、替代关系还是错误？ - **结论**：**关系不明确，无法确定**。现有证据中**未包含**官方数据手册（如 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）的直接内容，无法确认两型号的官方关系。 - **来源说明**： - 虽然检索了文件 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`，但所有证据均来自网表或接口文档，**未提取到该数据手册的文本内容**。 - 证据 [1] 至 [6] 显示网表使用 `TC367`，而证据 [8]、[9] 显示接口文档使用 `TC377`。两者在文档中**没有交叉引用**，也没有说明TC367与TC377的关系。 - **缺失信息**：**缺失官方数据手册内容的证据**，无法判断 `TC367` 和 `TC377` 是同一芯片的不同封装/型号、是替代关系，还是文档间的笔误。 ### 子问题 4：结合BOM表和设计变更记录，是否有型号变更通知？ - **结论**：**未发现型号变更通知**。BOM表和设计变更记录中均未提及从TC367变更到TC377或反之的过程。 - **来源说明**： - 证据 [19] 是接口文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的模板变更历史，记录了文档模板的版本迭代，但**未涉及芯片型号的变更**。 - 证据 [7] 至 [18] 均为接口文档中各模块的模式定义，未提及芯片型号变更。 - 证据 [17] 的“Change History2”表格内容为空，未提供有效信息。 - **缺失信息**：**缺失BOM表**和**设计变更记录**。未检索到BOM表文件，也未在接口文档中找到芯片型号变更的正式通知。 ### 证据冲突与处理建议 - **证据冲突**：根据检索账本中的 `conflicts` 列表，除了上述TC367和TC377的型号不一致外，接口文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 内部还存在大量字段冲突，例如 `tcan1044a`、`mc`、`accuracy`、`mcu_vcc0v75_en`、`asil`、`wake up source` 等。这些冲突表明该文档本身可能存在多个版本或未统一的信息。 - **处理建议**： 1. **确认芯片型号**：需要与硬件设计工程师或芯片供应商确认，网表中的“TC367”与接口文档中的“TC377”是否是同一个设计。这可能是设计初期的临时命名（TC367）与最终选型（TC377）的不一致，也可能是不同文档间的笔误。 2. **核查官方数据手册**：查阅英飞凌（Infineon）官方发布的 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S` 和 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA` 数据手册，确认两者是否为同一系列的不同产品，以及引脚和功能是否兼容。 3. **统一文档命名**：建议在网表和接口文档中统一采用的芯片型号，避免造成混淆。 **总结**：当前证据指向网表使用 `TC367` 而接口文档使用 `TC377`，两者关系不明，且无型号变更记录。建议优先确认芯片实际型号并统一命名。

event时，PHY芯片将进行复位，在Reset期间将无法LINKUP，MDIO无法总线访问 待机模式 仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state hw-v1-joint-pmic-noisy-querysuccess0.2610.6310.857not_applicable0.4930.6000.5000.457not_applicable
问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节，回答不应混入无关摄像头或 CAN 结论。
实际回答：根据检索账本和提供的证据，现结合电路与HSI文档，对LP87702的供电输出和配置说明如下： ### 1. LP87702 的供电输出 **证据来源：** - 电路设计文档（`circuit:21:instance:U1700`，来自 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）仅确认了该器件为 `LP87702-Q1`（型号 `LP87702RRHBRQ1`，封装 `pqfn50p5x5x90-32`）。 - HSI文档（`document:e6627ad70c8cab97`， `document:b6943ae371d76077`， `document:5e69a562758e3536`，来自 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）提供了功能描述和连接关系。 **缺失信息：** 电路设计文档中**未提供**LP87702各个供电输出引脚的详细连接关系（如每个引脚的具体电压值、负载网络及其连接目标）。当前证据仅能确认其**输出网络**（电源轨）的名称，无法确认具体引脚及其连接。 **可确认的供电输出（基于HSI描述）：** 根据HSI文档，LP87702模块（编号F13）的功能定义是“VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。具体输出网络及其负载描述如下： - **VCC1V8**：用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。 - **VCC1V1**：用于EQ6、DDR供电。 - **VCC50**：用于CAN收发器供电。 **输入电源：** 该芯片的输入电压为 **VCC3V3**。 ### 2. LP87702 的配置参数 **证据来源：** HSI文档（`document:ea7b2234534382bf`， `document:b6943ae371d76077`， `document:5e69a562758e3536`， `document:d69d04d302326d46`， `document:e1cc3eaeb1e15088`， `document:a6f318d1053a4c7e`，来自 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）。 **可确认的配置参数如下：** - **运行模式**：LP87702支持 **SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE** 四种模式。 - **使能控制**： - **VCC50输出**：由MCU的 **P15.3** 引脚（信号名 `MCU_5V_EN`）控制使能。高电平使能VCC50电压输出，低电平关断。 - **VCC1V8和VCC1V1输出**：由MCU通过 **I2C** 通信（使用引脚 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5`）进行使能和输出电压设置。 - **I2C通信**：LP87702的I2C地址为 **0x08**。MCU通过I2C总线对其进行配置。 - **看门狗配置**：LP87702内置窗口看门狗，其参数由OTP文件配置。具体参数为：喂狗长周期 **600ms**，窗口 **10~30ms**，看门狗输出复位信号低电平持续 **10ms**。喂狗信号由MCU的 **P13.3** 引脚提供。 - **复位与中断信号**： - **复位**：MCU通过 **P12.1** 引脚（信号名 `MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1`）复位该芯片（低电平有效）。 - **中断**：LP87702的输出中断信号连接到MCU的 **P12.1** 引脚，低电平表示有中断输出。 - **寄存器配置**：HSI文档（`document:d69d04d302326d46`）提到“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”，但**未提供具体的寄存器设置值或表格内容**。 ### 3. 缺失信息 - **供电输出引脚与连接**：电路设计文档中缺少LP87702各输出引脚（如OUT1、OUT2、OUT3等）的具体连接图，无法确认每个输出网络（VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50）对应的具体引脚编号及负载电路。 - **具体寄存器配置值**：HSI文档仅提及需要配置寄存器，但未给出具体的寄存器地址、值或配置表。 - **负载电流能力**：所有证据中均未提及LP87702各输出通道的负载电流能力。 ### 4. 证据冲突 根据检索账本，存在以下字段取值冲突，但**冲突值均来源于同一HSI文档**（`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`），且冲突字段（如 `lp87702`, `i2c0_scl`, `mcu` 等）多为网络标签或信号名称，在不同表格中可能因描述方式不同而出现差异，未形成实质性矛盾。因此，**没有需要合并或排除的确定结论冲突**。
检索上下文：Instance U1700: LP87702-Q1, 20200424007, LP87702RRHBRQ1, pqfn50p5x5x90-32. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：E11; None：P12.1; None：P12.1/GTM_TIM4_IN1_6/GTM_TIM3_IN1_6/GETH_MDIOC/GTM_TOUT129/CAN00_TXD/GETH_MDIO; None：GPIO; None：MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1; None：/; None：I; None：LP87702的输出中断信号。 高：无中断信号 低：输出中断信号; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：QM(B); None：F13/POWER F14/POWER; None：MCU配置LP87702、TPS62872; MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：E14; None：P15.5; None：P15.5/GTM_TIM3_IN0_4/GTM_TIM2_IN0_4/QSPI2_MTSRA/GTM_TOUT76/QSPI2_MTSR/MSC0_EN0/I2C0_SDA; None：I2C0_SDAC; None：MCU_SDA_P15.5; None：/; None：IO; None：IIC_SDA信号 外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08 TPS62872地址：0x41; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A17; None：P15.4; None：P15.4/GTM_TIM3_IN7_4/GTM_TIM2_IN7_4/I2C0_SCLC/QSPI2_MRSTA/SCU_E_REQ0_0/GTM_TOUT75/QSPI2_MRST; None：I2C0_SCL; None：MCU_SCL_P15.4; None：/; None：O; None：IIC_CLK信号 外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08 TPS62872地址：0x41; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC.
